{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SRTEyyVzFkR7PLJD5cLgfi","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t## Description\n2\t\n3\tThere are multiple user experience and validation issues on the **Review settings -> Review scores** page when adding, modifying, or saving score categories.\n4\t\n5\t\n6\t## Observed Issues & Gaps (from Video Analysis)\n7\t\n8\t### 1. Missing Delete Button for Individual Score Rows\n9\t\n10\t* **Issue:** When adding a new score value field using the `+` icon, there is no corresponding `-` or trash icon next to the fields to remove them.\n11\t* **Impact:** Users are forced to leave fields empty if they change their mind, which leads to the next validation bugs.\n12\t\n13\t### 2. Missing Form Validation & Silent Drop on Empty Fields\n14\t\n15\t* **Issue:** If a user adds a score row, leaves both the *Score* (numeric) and *Label* fields completely blank, and clicks **Save**, the system gives no validation error — the empty row is silently dropped and not saved, which is fine. But if only ONE of the two fields is filled in (e.g. a label with no score, or a score with no label), the same silence applies: no validation error appears, and the half-filled row is neither rejected nor explained.\n16\t* **Expected Behavior:** A row left completely blank should keep being silently discarded without blocking the save. A row where only one of the two fields was filled in, however, must be refused with a clear inline validation message next to that specific row — never silently saved, and never silently dropped.\n17\t\n18\t### 3. Non-Descriptive Global Error Banner\n19\t\n20\t* **Issue:** When an actual validation error triggers (e.g., leaving a score empty but adding a text label like \"12\"), a generic flash message appears at the top: *\"We had trouble saving your input - Please see below for details.\"*\n21\t* **Impact:** There are **no inline details** or highlighted fields below. The user has to guess what went wrong.\n22\t\n23\t### 4. Lack of Strict Input Typing (Data Integrity)\n24\t\n25\t* **Issue:** The \"Scores\" number fields accept invalid input formats. In the video, the field accepted an emoji (👍) and strings, which then triggered the broken validation workflow upon saving.\n26\t* **Expected Behavior:** The numeric score field should strictly enforce `type=\"number\"` or equivalent validation to reject emojis and non-numeric characters before submission.\n27\t\n28\t### 5. Adding a Category and Leaving It Blank Silently Creates a Nameless Category\n29\t\n30\t* **Issue:** When a user clicks the **+** button to add a new score category, then changes their mind and leaves the new category's name (and its scores) blank, saving the page — even for a completely unrelated change — still creates and stores that blank category, with no name and no validation error.\n31\t* **Expected Behavior:** A newly-added category the user never actually filled in should be silently discarded on save, the same way a completely blank score row is discarded — it must never be persisted as a nameless record.\n32\t\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t## Attached visual evidence\n37\t\n38\t- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1.mp4` — bug_screenshot (the screen recording attached to the report)\n39\t- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_1.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 1 of that recording)\n40\t- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_2.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 2 of that recording)\n41\t- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_3.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 3 of that recording)\n42\t\n43\tThese files are available inside the container and are part of the bug report; read them before making changes.\n44\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"40bd26f4-0eea-44a2-8967-5821991a41e5","uuid":"cccca612-1a22-4ebf-b823-1772ad70d475","timestamp":"2026-08-13T12:45:19.207Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/task/instruction.md","content":"## Description\n\nThere are multiple user experience and validation issues on the **Review settings -> Review scores** page when adding, modifying, or saving score categories.\n\n\n## Observed Issues & Gaps (from Video Analysis)\n\n### 1. Missing Delete Button for Individual Score Rows\n\n* **Issue:** When adding a new score value field using the `+` icon, there is no corresponding `-` or trash icon next to the fields to remove them.\n* **Impact:** Users are forced to leave fields empty if they change their mind, which leads to the next validation bugs.\n\n### 2. Missing Form Validation & Silent Drop on Empty Fields\n\n* **Issue:** If a user adds a score row, leaves both the *Score* (numeric) and *Label* fields completely blank, and clicks **Save**, the system gives no validation error — the empty row is silently dropped and not saved, which is fine. But if only ONE of the two fields is filled in (e.g. a label with no score, or a score with no label), the same silence applies: no validation error appears, and the half-filled row is neither rejected nor explained.\n* **Expected Behavior:** A row left completely blank should keep being silently discarded without blocking the save. A row where only one of the two fields was filled in, however, must be refused with a clear inline validation message next to that specific row — never silently saved, and never silently dropped.\n\n### 3. Non-Descriptive Global Error Banner\n\n* **Issue:** When an actual validation error triggers (e.g., leaving a score empty but adding a text label like \"12\"), a generic flash message appears at the top: *\"We had trouble saving your input - Please see below for details.\"*\n* **Impact:** There are **no inline details** or highlighted fields below. The user has to guess what went wrong.\n\n### 4. Lack of Strict Input Typing (Data Integrity)\n\n* **Issue:** The \"Scores\" number fields accept invalid input formats. In the video, the field accepted an emoji (👍) and strings, which then triggered the broken validation workflow upon saving.\n* **Expected Behavior:** The numeric score field should strictly enforce `type=\"number\"` or equivalent validation to reject emojis and non-numeric characters before submission.\n\n### 5. Adding a Category and Leaving It Blank Silently Creates a Nameless Category\n\n* **Issue:** When a user clicks the **+** button to add a new score category, then changes their mind and leaves the new category's name (and its scores) blank, saving the page — even for a completely unrelated change — still creates and stores that blank category, with no name and no validation error.\n* **Expected Behavior:** A newly-added category the user never actually filled in should be silently discarded on save, the same way a completely blank score row is discarded — it must never be persisted as a nameless record.\n\n\n\n\n## Attached visual evidence\n\n- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1.mp4` — bug_screenshot (the screen recording attached to the report)\n- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_1.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 1 of that recording)\n- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_2.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 2 of that recording)\n- `/app/.harbor_assets/bug_screenshot_1_3.png` — bug_screenshot (frame 3 of that recording)\n\nThese files are available inside the container and are part of the bug report; read them before making changes.\n","numLines":44,"startLine":1,"totalLines":44}}}

That is ordinarily trivial, but some daemons\n108\t# hand the build a rootfs whose ROOT DIRECTORY is 0750:\n109\t#\n110\t#     drwxr-x---  1 root root  /\n111\t#\n112\t# `/` is then untraversable for every non-root uid, so such a process dies before\n113\t# it reaches main(), with the loader unable to resolve libc:\n114\t#\n115\t#     /bin/sh: error while loading shared libraries: libc.so.6:\n116\t#              cannot open shared object file: Permission denied\n117\t#\n118\t# `chmod a+rx /` repairs it — but a mode change on the rootfs ROOT DIRECTORY is\n119\t# not captured in a layer diff, so it is reverted at the start of the next RUN.\n120\t# (Verified: `RUN chmod 0750 / && ls -ld /` followed by `RUN ls -ld /` prints\n121\t# 0750 and then 0755.)  Two consequences, both load-bearing:\n122\t#\n123\t#   * the repair must happen in the SAME layer as the non-root process, so a bare\n124\t#     `USER postgres` stanza cannot work here no matter what precedes it;\n125\t#   * the shipped image keeps whatever `/` the daemon gives it, so the runtime\n126\t#     starter has to repeat the repair before it drops privileges.\n127\t#\n128\t# Hence this one helper, used by both the build steps below and by\n129\t# harbor_pg_start at verify time.  It is a no-op on a normal daemon.\n130\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n131\t    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n132\t    \"# Run \\\"\\$@\\\" as the postgres user.  Repairs an untraversable / first: see\" \\\n133\t    \"# the long comment in the Dockerfile for why this cannot be a USER stanza.\" \\\n134\t    \"set -eu\" \\\n135\t    \"chmod a+rx /\" \\\n136\t    \"exec setpriv --reuid=postgres --regid=postgres --clear-groups \\\"\\$@\\\"\" \\\n137\t    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres\n138\t\n139\t# Preflight: prove non-root execution works through the same path the cluster\n140\t# steps use, and say so plainly BEFORE initdb muddies the log.  A failure here\n141\t# with `/` already world-traversable in the listing means the restriction is\n142\t# imposed by the daemon or an LSM rather than by file modes — that is not fixable\n143\t# from this Dockerfile and belongs with whoever maintains the sandbox.\n144\tRUN echo \"== non-root preflight ==\" \\\n145\t && id postgres \\\n146\t && ls -ld / /usr/lib /usr/share /var/lib/postgresql \\\n147\t && if harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c 'echo NONROOT_OK'; \\\n152\t      echo \"  Report the modes printed above to the sandbox maintainers.\" >&2; \\\n153\t      exit 1; \\\n154\t    fi\n155\t\n156\tRUN harbor_as_postgres \"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)/initdb\" \\\n157\t      -D \"$PGDATA\" -A trust -U postgres --encoding=UTF8 --locale=C\n158\t\n159\t# Server tuning lives in postgresql.conf rather than in `pg_ctl -o`, so that the\n160\t# start command needs no nested quoting anywhere.  fsync/full_page_writes off is\n161\t# safe and much faster: the cluster is throwaway, recreated with every container.\n162\t#\n163\t# The extensions are installed into template1 as well as into the application\n164\t# database: pytest-django creates `test_pretix` from template1, and eventyay's\n165\t# test settings DISABLE MIGRATIONS (tests/testutils/settings.py) so the schema is\n166\t# built with `--run-syncdb` and the CreateExtension migrations never execute.\n167\t# Without pg_trgm the whole tickets suite dies on\n168\t# `operator class \"gin_trgm_ops\" does not exist`.\n169\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n170\t    \"listen_addresses = '127.0.0.1'\" \\\n171\t    \"port = 5432\" \\\n172\t    \"fsync = off\" \\\n173\t    \"full_page_writes = off\" \\\n174\t    \"synchronous_commit = off\" \\\n175\t    \"max_connections = 100\" \\\n176\t    >> \"$PGDATA/postgresql.conf\"\n177\tRUN PGBIN=\"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n178\t && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -l /logs/postgres.log -w start \\\n179\t && harbor_as_postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -c \"CREATE DATABASE pretix\" \\\n180\t && for _ext in pg_trgm btree_gin btree_gist unaccent citext hstore pgcrypto; do \\\n181\t      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d template1 -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n182\t      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d pretix    -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n183\t    done \\\n184\t && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -w stop\n185\t\n186\t# One-line starter that every eventyay test.sh calls before pytest.\n187\t# Idempotent, offline, and waits until the server actually accepts connections.\n188\t# `\\$` keeps the expansion for RUN TIME instead of build time.\n189\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n190\t    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n191\t    \"# Start the in-image PostgreSQL cluster (offline; loopback only). Idempotent.\" \\\n192\t    \"set -u\" \\\n193\t    \"PGBIN=\\$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n194\t    \"mkdir -p /var/run/postgresql /logs\" \\\n195\t    \"chown postgres:postgres /var/run/postgresql\" \\\n196\t    \"# The server must run as user postgres: PostgreSQL aborts on euid 0, and\" \\\n197\t    \"# harbor_as_postgres repairs an untraversable / on the way.  Fail loudly if\" \\\n198\t    \"# even that cannot execute, rather than after a 60-second pg_isready wait.\" \\\n199\t    \"harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c true || {\" \\\n200\t    \"  echo \\\"FATAL: cannot execute as postgres in this container; see \\\\\\`ls -ld /\\\\\\`.\\\" >&2\" \\\n201\t    \"  exit 1\" \\\n202\t    \"}\" \\\n203\t    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0\" \\\n204\t    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_ctl -D \\$PGDATA -l /logs/postgres.log -w start || true\" \\\n205\t    \"for _ in \\$(seq 1 60); do harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0; sleep 1; done\" \\\n206\t    \"echo \\\"postgres did not come up\\\" >&2\" \\\n207\t    \"exit 1\" \\\n208\t    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start\n209\t\n210\t# --- harness plumbing ---------------------------------------------------------\n211\t# Visual evidence from the issue, readable by the agent at /app/.harbor_assets/.\n212\t# tests/ and solution/ are intentionally NOT baked in — the harness mounts them\n213\t# at verify time.\n214\tRUN mkdir -p /logs /app/.harbor_assets\n215\tCOPY assets/ /app/.harbor_assets/\n216\t\n217\tWORKDIR /app\n218\tCMD [\"bash\"]\n219\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"40bd26f4-0eea-44a2-8967-5821991a41e5","uuid":"28df3633-0ad1-417d-922c-df2a9eb073dd","timestamp":"2026-08-13T12:45:22.861Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/task/environment/Dockerfile","content":"# =============================================================================\n# fossasia/eventyay — Harbor task image (DONOR RECIPE)\n#\n# eventyay is a single Django 6 monolith (a merge of pretix + pretalx + video)\n# living in the repo sub-directory `app/`. The project itself is installed EDITABLE (uv's default) so\n# the agent's edits under /app/app/eventyay take effect with no reinstall.\nENV UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/opt/venv \\\n    UV_LINK_MODE=copy \\\n    VIRTUAL_ENV=/opt/venv \\\n    PATH=/opt/venv/bin:$PATH\nWORKDIR /app/app\nRUN uv sync --frozen --all-extras --group test \\\n && python -c \"import django, pytest, pytest_django; print(django.get_version(), pytest.__version__)\"\n\n# Compile the shipped translations: several eventyay tests assert on translated\n# strings, and `manage.py compilemessages` is part of the repo's own `make all`.\nRUN python manage.py compilemessages -x en 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# --- in-image PostgreSQL ------------------------------------------------------\n# Cluster is created (and the application database pre-created) at BUILD time so\n# that verify time only has to `pg_ctl start` — an offline, loopback-only op.\n# `-A trust` is safe: the container is single-tenant and unreachable.\nENV PGDATA=/var/lib/postgresql/harbor \\\n    EVY_POSTGRES_HOST=127.0.0.1 \\\n    EVY_POSTGRES_PORT=5432 \\\n    EVY_POSTGRES_USER=postgres \\\n    EVY_POSTGRES_PASSWORD=postgres \\\n    EVY_POSTGRES_DB=pretix\n# /logs must exist before the cluster is first started: the starter script points\n# the server log at /logs/postgres.log.\nRUN mkdir -p /logs \"$PGDATA\" /var/run/postgresql \\\n && chmod 777 /logs \\\n && chown -R postgres:postgres \"$PGDATA\" /var/run/postgresql\n\n# --- running as `postgres` on a daemon that ships / as 0750 -------------------\n# PostgreSQL REFUSES to run as uid 0: initdb and the postmaster both abort on\n# `geteuid() == 0` and there is no override flag, so every cluster step below has\n# to run as the `postgres` user.  That is ordinarily trivial, but some daemons\n# hand the build a rootfs whose ROOT DIRECTORY is 0750:\n#\n#     drwxr-x---  1 root root  /\n#\n# `/` is then untraversable for every non-root uid, so such a process dies before\n# it reaches main(), with the loader unable to resolve libc:\n#\n#     /bin/sh: error while loading shared libraries: libc.so.6:\n#              cannot open shared object file: Permission denied\n#\n# `chmod a+rx /` repairs it — but a mode change on the rootfs ROOT DIRECTORY is\n# not captured in a layer diff, so it is reverted at the start of the next RUN.\n# (Verified: `RUN chmod 0750 / && ls -ld /` followed by `RUN ls -ld /` prints\n# 0750 and then 0755.)  Two consequences, both load-bearing:\n#\n#   * the repair must happen in the SAME layer as the non-root process, so a bare\n#     `USER postgres` stanza cannot work here no matter what precedes it;\n#   * the shipped image keeps whatever `/` the daemon gives it, so the runtime\n#     starter has to repeat the repair before it drops privileges.\n#\n# Hence this one helper, used by both the build steps below and by\n# harbor_pg_start at verify time.  It is a no-op on a normal daemon.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n    \"# Run \\\"\\$@\\\" as the postgres user.  Repairs an untraversable / first: see\" \\\n    \"# the long comment in the Dockerfile for why this cannot be a USER stanza.\" \\\n    \"set -eu\" \\\n    \"chmod a+rx /\" \\\n    \"exec setpriv --reuid=postgres --regid=postgres --clear-groups \\\"\\$@\\\"\" \\\n    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres\n\n# Preflight: prove non-root execution works through the same path the cluster\n# steps use, and say so plainly BEFORE initdb muddies the log.  A failure here\n# with `/` already world-traversable in the listing means the restriction is\n# imposed by the daemon or an LSM rather than by file modes — that is not fixable\n# from this Dockerfile and belongs with whoever maintains the sandbox.\nRUN echo \"== non-root preflight ==\" \\\n && id postgres \\\n && ls -ld / /usr/lib /usr/share /var/lib/postgresql \\\n && if harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c 'echo NONROOT_OK'; then \\\n      echo \"PREFLIGHT: non-root execution works\"; \\\n    else \\\n      echo \"NONROOT_STILL_BROKEN: cannot execute as postgres on this daemon.\" >&2; \\\n      echo \"  PostgreSQL refuses to run as uid 0, so this image cannot build here.\" >&2; \\\n      echo \"  Report the modes printed above to the sandbox maintainers.\" >&2; \\\n      exit 1; \\\n    fi\n\nRUN harbor_as_postgres \"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)/initdb\" \\\n      -D \"$PGDATA\" -A trust -U postgres --encoding=UTF8 --locale=C\n\n# Server tuning lives in postgresql.conf rather than in `pg_ctl -o`, so that the\n# start command needs no nested quoting anywhere.  fsync/full_page_writes off is\n# safe and much faster: the cluster is throwaway, recreated with every container.\n#\n# The extensions are installed into template1 as well as into the application\n# database: pytest-django creates `test_pretix` from template1, and eventyay's\n# test settings DISABLE MIGRATIONS (tests/testutils/settings.py) so the schema is\n# built with `--run-syncdb` and the CreateExtension migrations never execute.\n# Without pg_trgm the whole tickets suite dies on\n# `operator class \"gin_trgm_ops\" does not exist`.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"listen_addresses = '127.0.0.1'\" \\\n    \"port = 5432\" \\\n    \"fsync = off\" \\\n    \"full_page_writes = off\" \\\n    \"synchronous_commit = off\" \\\n    \"max_connections = 100\" \\\n    >> \"$PGDATA/postgresql.conf\"\nRUN PGBIN=\"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -l /logs/postgres.log -w start \\\n && harbor_as_postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -c \"CREATE DATABASE pretix\" \\\n && for _ext in pg_trgm btree_gin btree_gist unaccent citext hstore pgcrypto; do \\\n      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d template1 -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d pretix    -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n    done \\\n && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -w stop\n\n# One-line starter that every eventyay test.sh calls before pytest.\n# Idempotent, offline, and waits until the server actually accepts connections.\n# `\\$` keeps the expansion for RUN TIME instead of build time.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n    \"# Start the in-image PostgreSQL cluster (offline; loopback only). Idempotent.\" \\\n    \"set -u\" \\\n    \"PGBIN=\\$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n    \"mkdir -p /var/run/postgresql /logs\" \\\n    \"chown postgres:postgres /var/run/postgresql\" \\\n    \"# The server must run as user postgres: PostgreSQL aborts on euid 0, and\" \\\n    \"# harbor_as_postgres repairs an untraversable / on the way.  Fail loudly if\" \\\n    \"# even that cannot execute, rather than after a 60-second pg_isready wait.\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c true || {\" \\\n    \"  echo \\\"FATAL: cannot execute as postgres in this container; see \\\\\\`ls -ld /\\\\\\`.\\\" >&2\" \\\n    \"  exit 1\" \\\n    \"}\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_ctl -D \\$PGDATA -l /logs/postgres.log -w start || true\" \\\n    \"for _ in \\$(seq 1 60); do harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0; sleep 1; done\" \\\n    \"echo \\\"postgres did not come up\\\" >&2\" \\\n    \"exit 1\" \\\n    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start\n\n# --- harness plumbing ---------------------------------------------------------\n# Visual evidence from the issue, readable by the agent at /app/.harbor_assets/.\n# tests/ and solution/ are intentionally NOT baked in — the harness mounts them\n# at verify time.\nRUN mkdir -p /logs /app/.harbor_assets\nCOPY assets/ /app/.harbor_assets/\n\nWORKDIR /app\nCMD [\"bash\"]\n","numLines":219,"startLine":1,"totalLines":219}}}

The submitted payload is not\n+hand-written: it is HARVESTED from the page's own rendered controls, mutated,\n+and posted back, exactly as a browser would.  No module, class, function,\n+attribute, template, CSS class, message text or form-field name introduced by\n+any particular repair is referenced anywhere below.\n+\n+Two independent observation channels are used:\n+\n+  * what the organiser is TOLD -- a report is attached to the score row that is\n+    actually wrong, and to no other row.  \"Attached to the row\" is measured\n+    structurally: the smallest part of the page that holds that row's controls\n+    and no other control, compared against the same part of a healthy render.\n+  * what the database HOLDS afterwards -- a rejected submission must change\n+    nothing, an accepted one must change exactly what was asked for.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+import datetime as dt\n+import re\n+\n+import pytest\n+from django.test import Client\n+from django.utils.datastructures import MultiValueDict\n+from django_scopes import scope, scopes_disabled\n+\n+import lxml.html\n+\n+from eventyay.base.models import (\n+    Event,\n+    Organizer,\n+    ReviewScore,\n+    ReviewScoreCategory,\n+    Team,\n+    User,\n+)\n+\n+\n+pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db\n+\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# The name-lock canary.\n+#\n+# Every symbol, CSS class and message the upstream repair introduces is planted\n+# in the FIXTURE, as ordinary user data, so all of them are already present in\n+# the page on the UNREPAIRED tree. _settings_form(doc):\n+    for form in doc.iter('form'):\n+        for element in form.iter('input'):\n+            if element.get('name') == _TOTAL:\n+                return form\n+    raise AssertionError('the review settings page offered no score formset')\n+\n+\n+def _controls(form):\n+    \"\"\"Every submittable control of the form, in document order.\"\"\"\n+    for element in form.iter():\n+        if element.tag not in ('input', 'select', 'textarea'):\n+            continue\n+        name = element.get('name')\n+        if not name or '__prefix__' in name:\n+            continue\n+        yield element, name\n+\n+\n+def _collect(root):\n+    \"\"\"What a browser would submit for every control under ``root``.\"\"\"\n+    data = {}\n+\n+    def add(name, value):\n+        data.setdefault(name, []).append(value)\n+\n+    for element, name in _controls(root):\n+        if element.tag == 'input':\n+            kind = (element.get('type') or 'text').lower()\n+            if kind in ('submit', 'button', 'reset', 'image', 'file'):\n+                continue\n+            if kind in ('checkbox', 'radio'):\n+                if element.get('checked') is not None:\n+                    add(name, element.get('value') or 'on')\n+            else:\n+                add(name, element.get('value') or '')\n+        elif element.tag == 'textarea':\n+            text = \"\"\"Every enclosing part of the page, outermost first, ending with the node itself.\"\"\"\n+    parts = path.split('/')\n+    return ['/'.join(parts[: index + 1]) for index in range(1,  holds this row's controls and no other control at all.  Whatever text that\n+    part carries is what the page says about this row -- wherever a repair\n+    chooses to put it, and whatever it chooses to call it.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    doc = _tree(html)\n+      \"\"\"Category slot -> [(score key, number control, label control)] as shown.\"\"\"\n+        html = html if html is not None else self.show()\n+        found = {}\n+        for (slot, key), found.setdefault(slot, []).append((key, number, label))\n+        return found\n+\n+    def row_for(self, label_text):\n+        \"\"\"The (slot, key, number control, label control) of the row showing    return match.group(1)\n+        raise AssertionError(f'no score category on the page is named {name_text!r}')\n+\n+    def added_row_names(self, slot, key):\n+        return
